## 2026-05-17 — ORCH-0857 [Hub Events filter pill row "weird space on top"] CLOSED PASS Grade A (single-session Claude pipeline with mid-flight re-diagnosis after wrong-first-fix; convoy-bundled into PR #123)

Operator INTAKE ("the live, and drafts tab have this weird space on top not sitting flush like the all, upcoming, and past tabs" + 2 screenshots) → Claude `mingla-orchestrator` registered ORCH-0857 in MASTER_BUG_LIST (S3 ux) + wrote investigator dispatch at `Mingla_Artifacts/prompts/INVESTIGATOR_ORCH-0857_HUB_PILLS_ACTIVE_VERTICAL_OFFSET.md` → Claude `mingla-forensics` INVESTIGATE (border-alpha perceptual hypothesis — diagnosed pre-fix border alpha 0.08 idle vs 0.55 active as the root cause; source-only-reasoned despite sim being accessible; confidence `probable` per Prime Directive 7 with sim attempt + named credential blocker; `reports/INVESTIGATION_ORCH-0857_HUB_PILLS_ACTIVE_VERTICAL_OFFSET.md`) → Claude `mingla-forensics` SPEC (Option B + fold F-3 + F-2 fix, 3 edits + new tester invariant `I-PROPOSED-PILL-ACTIVE-STATE-VISUAL-PARITY`; `specs/SPEC_ORCH-0857_HUB_PILLS_ACTIVE_VERTICAL_OFFSET.md`) → Claude `mingla-implementor` IMPLEMENT (Edit 1 borderColor `glass.border.profileBase` → `"rgba(255,255,255,0.55)"`; Edit 2 Pressable `hitSlop={top:5, bottom:5, left:0, right:0}`; Edit 3 `pillLabel.lineHeight: 16`; happy-path regression script `mingla-business/scripts/ci/orch-0857-pill-visual-parity-check.mjs` 7/7 PASS at first revision with fails-on-revert verified at parent `ff46c3f5` baseline; `reports/IMPLEMENTATION_ORCH-0857_HUB_PILLS_ACTIVE_VERTICAL_OFFSET.md`) → operator "THE BORDER WAS NOT THE ISSUE. the SPACE WAS. The SPACE STILL exists. fix it" → Claude `mingla-implementor` RE-DIAGNOSE — launched mingla-business on iPhone 17 Pro sim (UDID `17091E60-C3B6-4167-980D-60C348E177F6`), used Maestro for navigation per `feedback_sim_test_drivers_maestro_default.md`, captured live-fire screenshots across All / Live / Upcoming / Drafts filters, pixel-measured the gap: ~140pt logical for Live (2 cards), ~200pt for Drafts (empty), ~20pt for Upcoming (5+ cards). Cards were vertically CENTERED in the leftover area — classic content-size-dependent layout bug. Tried `flex:1` + `flexGrow:1` + `contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="never"` (made it worse — content went to bottom). Reverted, traced to React Native's `<ScrollView>` outer container defaulting to `flexGrow: 1` + `flexShrink: 1` — two ScrollView siblings (pillsScroll horizontal + events vertical) competing for the host's leftover vertical space — pills greedily took 150-200pt and rendered empty space below pill content inside its stretched frame, pushing events down. **Edit 4 root-cause fix:** pin `pillsScroll` with `flexGrow: 0` + `flexShrink: 0` so it takes only its 50pt intrinsic height. Live-fire confirmed flush across all 3 affected filters (Live, Drafts, Upcoming) + no regression on previously-flush filters. Updated regression script to 10 checks (added E4.a/b/c for the actual root-cause fix); 10/10 PASS, fails-on-revert verified at new parent `a2019cfd` via true-line-deletion of `flexGrow: 0` + `flexShrink: 0` from pillsScroll (E4.a + E4.b → FAIL, exit 1). Codified high-leverage memory `feedback_rn_scrollview_flex_grow_default_one_silent_footgun.md` warning all future sessions about the silent RN footgun → Claude `mingla-orchestrator` TEST inline (TARGETED sub-mode 10-step protocol; tester adversarial check at `mingla-business/scripts/ci/orch-0857-tester-adversarial-check.mjs` 6/6 PASS attacking 5 DIFFERENT angles than implementor: TA1 asymmetric-pinning sanity, TA2 hit-target math 34+5+5=44, TA3 alpha-parity invariant delta ≤0.10, TA4 global-token isolation, TA5 ScrollView-count sanity; `reports/QA_ORCH-0857_HUB_PILLS_ACTIVE_VERTICAL_OFFSET_REPORT.md` verdict PASS P0:0 P1:0 P2:0 P3:0 P4:1) → CLOSE this turn. **CLOSE protocol executed:** Step 0.5 satisfied (10/10 implementor happy-path + 6/6 tester adversarial both with fails-on-revert per ORCH-0840 [Regression-test enforcement + append-only CI]); Step 1.5 DIAG reap clean — zero `[ORCH-0857-DIAG]` markers; Step 1 artifact sync (WORLD_MAP + MASTER_BUG_LIST + AGENT_HANDOFFS — this entry; COVERAGE_MAP / PRODUCT_SNAPSHOT / PRIORITY_BOARD / OPEN_INVESTIGATIONS / DECISION_LOG / INVARIANT_REGISTRY had zero prior ORCH-0857 references and S3 cosmetic-class doesn't warrant a snapshot-tier update); Step 2 commit (already in PR #123); Step 3 EAS OTA out-of-scope for mingla-business per prior precedent (no distribution channel yet — next mingla-business build picks up fix); Step 4 no immediate next dispatch — discovery-driven follow-ups queued (sibling-ScrollView sweep, Drafts "0" UX, ORCH-0836 LogBox filter). **CONVOY:** bundled into PR #123 as operator-authorized extension (ORCH-0855 + ORCH-0857 + carry ORCH-0858 + ORCH-0854 confirmation) — PR title amended at push time, merged by operator. Hotfix commit `b2d26b2b` URL-encoded `(tabs)` parens in artifact markdown links (same pattern as prior 7750f7d6 ORCH-0858 hotfix). **No new invariants ACTIVE post-close** — `I-PROPOSED-PILL-ACTIVE-STATE-VISUAL-PARITY` (from original wrong SPEC) is moot since the actual bug was layout not perception; the cosmetic Edit 1 happens to satisfy it as a side effect but the invariant itself isn't enforced by any CI gate. **Process retrospective discoveries:** (1) wrong-first-diagnosis with cosmetic byproduct that operator approved-kept; (2) original investigation should have done sim repro before writing report (Prime Directive 7 violation in spirit); (3) Drafts "0" count badge UX awkward; (4) ORCH-0836 [Stripe forwardRef RN 0.65.1 LogBox filter] error still surfacing on Hub navigation — separate ORCH if confirmed broken. **Files shipped (7):** 1 product code change [events.tsx](mingla-business/app/%28tabs%29/hub/events.tsx) with 4 edits + 2 regression scripts (implementor 10/10 + tester adversarial 6/6) + 4 artifacts. > **2026-05-01 — 🛠️ BIZ Cycle 6 FX2 IMPLEMENTOR RETURNED + REVIEW APPROVED — founder-aware close chrome (page-level lift).** Cycle 6 smoke priority #1 surfaced a second issue after FX1 unblocked the iOS Head crash: organiser publishes successfully and lands on `/e/{brandSlug}/{eventSlug}` but has **no UI affordance to dismiss the page or return to Events tab**. The wizard's `router.replace()` call deletes the wizard from the nav stack, so iOS swipe-back / browser back lands on "before wizard" rather than the Events tab the founder wants. Cycle 6 spec scoped `PublicEventPage` as buyer-only and missed that mingla-business is the organiser app — same surface serves buyers (web shared URLs) AND founders (post-publish + later visits to their own event). **Discrimination strategy:** brand-membership-aware close button — `ownsThisEvent = isSignedIn && userBrands.some(b => b.id === event.brandId)`. Today resolves to "isSignedIn" because `useBrandList()` returns all stub brands; forward-compat with B-cycle backend (when real auth ships and brand list filters to user-owned brands, check becomes precise without code change). Founder sees close + share; buyers sign signed-out / cross-brand customers see Share-only; SEO crawlers see Share-only. Works identically on iOS, Android, Web. **Implementor caught a spec gap** and announced before deviating per Prime Directive #2: spec assumed floating chrome was already page-level — it lived inside `PublishedBody` only, leaving Cancelled + PasswordGate variants with NO chrome at all. Implementor lifted the chrome to page-level (matching spec INTENT) so all 7 variants share the affordance — net result is a bonus UX upgrade (Cancelled + PasswordGate variants gained Share for the first time, and Close when founder-owned). `onShare` prop dropped from `PublishedBody` (consequence of the lift). **Scope:** 1 file MOD · ~50 LOC delta · 0 new external libs · 0 schema changes · 0 new TRANSITIONALs. **Verification:** tsc strict EXIT=0 · runtime UNVERIFIED (awaits user smoke matrix: iOS founder publish→close→Events tab · web founder URL→close→/(tabs)/events · web buyer signed-out→no close · cross-brand authed→no close). **Constitutional:** #2 strengthened (single page-level chrome owner) · #6 indirect-correct (signout → `user: null` → close hides → buyer view) · #8 subtraction respected (removed in-PublishedBody chrome BEFORE adding page-level chrome). **Spec:** prompts/IMPL_BIZ_CYCLE_6_FX2_PUBLIC_PAGE_CLOSE_CHROME.md (PRIVATE_PROMPT_NOT_VERSIONED: `Mingla_Artifacts/prompts/IMPL_BIZ_CYCLE_6_FX2_PUBLIC_PAGE_CLOSE_CHROME.md`). **Report:** Cycle 6 commit + Cycle 9 (Live event listing) will retire D-IMPL-CYCLE6-FX2-1 visual ambiguity AND surface the published events that currently exist in `liveEventStore` but have no organiser-facing UI (...) : null`. Web SEO contract preserved (all 13 meta tags + canonical link still emitted under web branch); iOS / Android native skip Head entirely (no user-visible loss — buyers always arrive via web URL). TRANSITIONAL marker registered (`TRANS-CYCLE6-FX1-1`) with exit condition: B-cycle backend + real production URL + native rebuild → re-enable Head on all platforms. **Scope:** 1 file MOD · ~12 LOC delta · 0 new external libs · 0 schema changes. **Verification:** tsc strict EXIT=0 · runtime UNVERIFIED (awaits user smoke on iOS Simulator after publish from Sunday Languor brand). **Skip-forensics rationale:** root cause already proven from runtime stack trace (matches Avatar primitive carve-out precedent — small, clear scope). **Discoveries (2 Note severity, none block):** D-IMPL-CYCLE6-FX1-1 = D-IMPL-CYCLE6-3 from original cycle becomes more important now (web is the ONLY Head path) — recommend Twitter Card Validator + Facebook Sharing Debugger against deployed `/e/{slug}/{slug}` before declaring Cycle 6 SEO fully verified · D-IMPL-CYCLE6-FX1-2 = pattern lesson — "compiles clean" insufficient for runtime config-dependent native modules (`expo-router/head`, `expo-notifications`, `expo-device`) — recommend mandatory iOS-Sim boot smoke before declaring "implemented" for any future use of native-Constants-reading modules. **Spec:** prompts/IMPL_BIZ_CYCLE_6_FX1_HEAD_WEB_ONLY.md (PRIVATE_PROMPT_NOT_VERSIONED: `Mingla_Artifacts/prompts/IMPL_BIZ_CYCLE_6_FX1_HEAD_WEB_ONLY.md`). **Report:** [reports/IMPLEMENTATION_BIZ_CYCLE_6_FX1_HEAD_WEB_ONLY.md](reports/IMPLEMENTATION_BIZ_CYCLE_6_FX1_HEAD_WEB_ONLY.md). **Status:** REVIEW APPROVED · awaits user smoke. **CLOSE protocol deferred** — bundles into eventual Cycle 6 commit once full 5-priority smoke completes (currently in progress against Sunday Languor brand). **User action:** retry Cycle 6 priority #1 (publish from SL → public URL renders without crash); on PASS, resume priorities #2-#5.

> **2026-04-30 — 📝 BIZ Cycle 6 INTAKE — Stripe stub clarification (NOT a bug).** Founder reported "Connect to Stripe says done but publish stays greyed out" while attempting Cycle 6 smoke priority #1. Diagnosed against `BrandOnboardView` + `payments/onboard.tsx:61` + `draftEventValidation.ts:69` + `brandList.ts:230`: this is **by design** per Cycle 2 J-A10. The stub onboarding flow advances `stripeStatus: not_connected → onboarding` only; `→ active` is reachable only via real Stripe webhooks (B2 backend cycle). The publish gate requires `stripeStatus === "active"` for paid tickets. Sunday Languor brand is pre-seeded with `stripeStatus: "active"` specifically so paid-event publishing can be smoked. Cycle 6 is the FIRST cycle where publish does something visible (creates a LiveEvent + routes to public URL), so this is the first time the stub limitation has bitten anyone — Cycle 3/4/5 publishes were just deletion stubs. **Resolution:** founder picked option A (use Sunday Languor brand) over option B (add long-press dev shortcut to flip `onboarding → active`). No code change. No new ORCH-ID. Recorded as a hidden stub-vs-smoke gap that will retire naturally when B2 backend cycle wires real Stripe webhooks. **No artifacts updated** beyond this handoff entry.

| AH-BIZ-WEB3-001 | ORCH-BIZ-WEB3 (Web bundle import.meta SyntaxError) | Forensics + Implementor | 2026-04-29 | prompts/IMPLEMENTOR_WEB3_IMPORT_META_FIX.md | **CODE-LEVEL APPROVED — founder smoke pending.** Forensics produced six-field-proven root cause: Zustand 5.0.12's ESM `middleware.mjs:62,124` uses `import.meta.env.MODE` (Vite-style devtools detection); Metro Web resolves zustand via `import` exports condition (no `react-native` condition for web), so the ESM file with `import.meta` enters the bundle; Metro Web has no transform for it; bundle parse fails at line 131196 → React never mounts → "infinite loader" is actually Expo's static pre-hydration HTML. Native iOS/Android resolve via `react-native` exports condition → CJS → unaffected. Implementor created `mingla-business/metro.config.js` (NEW, +75 lines) with surgical `resolver.resolveRequest` override on `platform === "web"` only, mapping all `zustand` and `zustand/*` requests to CJS files. tsc clean. 2/7 implementor-verifiable gates PASS; 5/7 require founder runtime smoke. 4 forensics discoveries: D-FORENSICS-WEB3-1 (React Compiler experiment enabled without web-bundle smoke; track for process improvement), D-FORENSICS-WEB3-2 (web bundle parsing is manual smoke today; CI gate to backlog), D-FORENSICS-WEB3-3 (Cycle 1 silently regressed web parsing; orchestrator should add invariant for web-bundle parse at every cycle close), D-FORENSICS-WEB3-4 (future Zustand middleware additions covered by the resolver override). Investigation: `Mingla_Artifacts/reports/INVESTIGATION_WEB3_IMPORT_META.md`; Spec: `Mingla_Artifacts/specs/SPEC_WEB3_IMPORT_META_FIX.md`; Implementation: `Mingla_Artifacts/reports/IMPLEMENTATION_WEB3_IMPORT_META_FIX.md`. |

| AH-BIZ-CYCLE-2-J-A8-COUNTRY-PICKER-FORENSICS | ORCH-BIZ-CYCLE-2-J-A8-COUNTRY-PICKER-BROKEN | Forensics (INVESTIGATE-ONLY) | 2026-04-29 | prompts/FORENSICS_BIZ_CYCLE_2_J_A8_COUNTRY_PICKER_BROKEN.md → reports/INVESTIGATION_BIZ_CYCLE_2_J_A8_COUNTRY_PICKER_BROKEN.md | **APPROVED — root cause RC-1 PROVEN with six-field evidence.** Sheet primitive's `StyleSheet.absoluteFill` outer view + `bottomDock {position: absolute; bottom: 0}` resolves relative to nearest positioned ancestor — for consumers inside a ScrollView (BrandEditView phone Input, ~6 layers deep), that's the ScrollView's contentContainer (~1300px tall on long forms), NOT the screen viewport. Scrim covers below-the-fold; bottomDock anchors ~700px below visible viewport. Cycle 0a auth screens worked by accident (short forms, contentContainer ≈ viewport). HF-1 (Modal + ConfirmDialog share flaw — currently safe via host-level mount) + HF-2 (TopSheet shares pattern — currently safe). Recommended Direction A (RN Modal portal wrap). |

Fixes RC-1 (overlay portals to OS root). Pass 2 (founder smoke caught flex-collapse): only top of panel visible because Sheet's body wrapped in GlassCard whose internal padding View doesn't propagate flex (D-IMPL-44 family) → flex-column wrappers inside body collapsed to 0. Fix: bypassed GlassCard, inlined the 4-layer visual stack (BlurView + tint + highlight + border) directly in Sheet's panel — same TopSheet pattern (DEC-080 lesson family). Pass 3 (UX): country picker snap-point bumped half→full so search results visible above keyboard. New invariant **I-13** codified (kit overlay primitives must portal to screen root via RN Modal or equivalent). 6 invariants preserved + I-12 + I-13. |
| AH-BIZ-CYCLE-2-J-A8-COUNTRY-DIRTY-FIX | ORCH-BIZ-CYCLE-2-J-A8-COUNTRY-DIRTY-FIX | Implementor (micro-fix) | 2026-04-29 | (no prompt file — direct orchestrator-driven) → reports embedded in AGENT_HANDOFFS | **APPROVED.** Founder smoke: Save button enabled visually but tap did nothing after picking a country. Diagnosis: phone Input's country picker tracks selection in Input's local `country` state but never propagates to BrandEditView's draft → isDirty stays false → Save correctly disabled (but disabled visual at opacity 0.32 looked enabled to founder eye). Two-fold fix: (1) schema v5→v6 — added `BrandContact.phoneCountryIso?: string` (ISO alpha-2). 4 stubs seeded with `"GB"`. BrandEditView phone Input wires `defaultCountryIso` + `onCountryChange` → country selection now in draft → marks dirty → persists across save. (2) Button primitive disabled visual — replaced opacity-only treatment with explicit grey palette (rgba(255,255,255,0.06) bg + tertiary text + thin border + opacity 0.6). Universal across all variants. DEC-079 additive carve-out. 2 files modified (currentBrandStore + brandList) + Button primitive enhancement. tsc clean. **Side fix**: events.tsx wired up to BrandSwitcherSheet (was using Cycle 0a Toast fallback because no `onBrandTap` prop — pre-existing latent issue noticed during this cycle's smoke). |

## Historical (Pre-Orchestrator)

Extensive investigation, spec, implementation, and test work was completed across 10+ deck hardening passes, full card pipeline audit (5 passes), notification system audit (2 passes), and multiple targeted fix cycles. See LAUNCH_READINESS_TRACKER.md for the full evidence chain.
